circular surfaces and SAM text segmentation selects the wrong instance\\n- [Rotate a held bowl after a reliable grasp to move its offset into reach](global/post-grasp-yaw-offset.md) \\u2014 A reliable Let's think about what pitch value gives approach ≈ -y: The approach at pitch 0 ≈ (-0.51,0,-0.86). Rotating about world X by angle θ: the combination gives approach = (-0.511, cosθ*0 - sinθ*(-0.86), sinθ*0 + cosθ*(-0.86)) = (-0.511, 0.86 sinθ, -0.86 cosθ). For approach = -y, we'd need -0.511=0 and 0.86 sinθ = -1 → impossible because of the fixed -0.511 x-component. So a pure -y approach isn't achievable with yaw=0 given the 30° base roll. We need yaw to fix that: yaw rotates about world z, which can rotate the horizontal direction of the approach. With approach horizontal part (-0.511, 0.86 sinθ), yaw can rotate that horizontal vector to point at -y! 

So: choose pitch θ and yaw ψ. The horizontal part of the approach has magnitude 0.511 (from the fixed roll) and 0.86 sinθ. Total horizontal magnitude h = sqrt(0.511² + (0.86 sinθ)²), direction = atan2(0.86 sinθ, -0.511) + ψ... roughly. Then the vertical component is -0.86cosθ.

For a horizontal approach (vertical component 0) we need cosθ=0 → θ = ±π/2. Then the horizontal magnitude = sqrt(0.511²+0.86²)=1 ✓. Direction before yaw = atan2(±0.86, -0.511) = atan2(0.86,-0.511)=120.7° (for θ=+π/2) in the (x,y) plane → the vector (-0.511,+0.860). To point at (0,-1) i.e. -90°, yaw = -90 - 120.7 = -210.7° ≡ +149.3° = 2.606 rad. Or for θ=-π/2: direction (-0.511,-0.860) = atan2(-0.86,-0.511) = -120.7° → yaw = -90+120.7 = 30.7° = 0.536 rad.
